# The Mark of the Beast

**[00:00:00]** The book of Revelation prophesies in the end times that people will be offered a mark of the beast in exchange for their soul.

**[00:00:07]** This mark is going to give them worldly comforts of being able to buy and sell and participate in society,

**[00:00:12]** and many people will be deceived.

**[00:00:14]** But is this mark, which many have speculated about, a physical reality, or is it a spiritual one?

**[00:00:21]** That's up next on the Dance of Life.

**[00:00:30]** Hey everybody, welcome back to the Dance of Life podcast.

**[00:00:35]** Thanks so much for being with me today.

**[00:00:36]** I'm always happy to be with you.

**[00:00:38]** If you haven't yet subscribed, make sure you do so on my website, because you never know with these platforms.

**[00:00:44]** I like to say that, especially in the topic of the end times, which we are studying.

**[00:00:48]** There's a lot of controversial things, and of course, when you start talking about controversial things, then you just never know.

**[00:00:55]** So ultimately, stay tuned through my website if all this stuff is interesting to you.

**[00:01:00]** That's the best way to stay in touch.

**[00:01:01]** I probably reach out to people maybe once or twice per week, depending on what kind of content I'm doing.

**[00:01:06]** But either way, that's the safest.

**[00:01:08]** And either way, I'm happy to be with you today, happy to share all this very important information,
